#### U0c · The scaffold, in two sittings (the game looks and plays the same in every old place)

**U0c1, the places** (the four files U0b had): the other seven places on stand-ins, with exits both ways, labels, pins placed clear of their neighbours on today's area pictures, doors (4.1, 4.2: the Bond clause for the two girls' rooms and for the corridor after dark, "the house first" with its two exceptions, "a house is always open from one of its own rooms", the pool at Night) and the rest of 4.3's cells, the two girls' and the teachers' Nights and Maki's weekend Evening among them; the other rows of `WORLD_HOMES` (Nobara, Maki, Yaga, Utahime, Ijichi), each with that person's name on the asleep lists in the same commit, and `world_room_state`; `WORLD_AWAY` and `world_away`, with the Sunday afternoons and the one line in `advance_period` that lets a companion go when their Tokyo hour, or the hour before it, begins (7.6) (until wave 1 the journal says "Off campus" for the three on Sunday afternoons and a ride finds nobody: the checkpoint A note says so; naming the district is U1's); the old yards' code points left on today's sides (K12); as its last step, `tools/art/game_layout.json` regenerated (5.4). *Acceptance:* the standard set; from `dorm` every one of 35 places is reached by exits; a save made in each new place reloads there; `p2_paths.rpy:739-740` passes unedited on the schematic exits; **walk into Nobara's room in the Afternoon as her companion, with no Bond, Wait to Evening, walk out: the exit works;** on every one of seven Nights everyone who lives on campus is in their own home and the garden and the Common Room are empty; Utahime and Ijichi are in the Teachers' Dormitory, asleep, and not on its stage.

**U0c2, the readers, gates, hooks and stubs:** `WORLD_VARIANTS` and its registration (a fresh row replaces a static cell); the manifest reader and the four gates (5.5); `rule_layout`'s `for_rev`; `path_validate`'s spacing on applied points; `path_keep_clear`; hover-label sides from `tips` or from position; four- or six-value light rows and the `light_` and `light.json` readers; the sidecar keys of 6.1 read with today's behaviour as default; "Show names" and the held N key in `ui_icons.rpy` and `sb_hotspots.rpy`; `SPOT_PLANNED` and the generic `dim` state in `sb_hotspots.rpy`; **the shapes of D60, final here** (7.3): `hot_shapes_for` (the `shapes` key, read only from a sidecar that is not stale and only for a place whose picture is at revision 2 or later, by the picture's file stem); in `hot_layer`, for a shaped hotspot, the square drawn empty inside today's alpha wrapper, with its id, action and label unchanged and a focus mask that refuses every point, the shape's focus area (`hotshape_<id>`, with its own tooltip key, skipped by the keyboard) and its light (a plain top-level class and a curried top-level function, so that an instance of each pickles; no function transform; drawn as 7.3 says, so that it shows on white and on black), lit whenever the tooltip in force is the hotspot's under either key, the dimmed strengths with `dim`'s reason, every shape lit with its name while names are shown, the smaller shape on top within a layer, and any visible hotspot without a shape drawn as its button; `hover_label` opening against `hot_tip_rect` and drawing a house's faces beside a shaped exit's name; the reader of `hot_look.json`; the shape rules in `hot_validate`, with the one counting function 7.3 names; `shapes`, `keep_tile` and `hot_look.json` in `check_content.py --layout-files`; the limits and each place's shapes in the dump (`u2_dump.rpy`); **and the harness lines of 10.3's D60 rows turned into rules that hold in a place with buttons and in a shaped one**; `pool_swim` and `swim_pool` in `NIGHT_REST_BLOCKS`, with the `swim_pool` row appended to `ACTIVITIES` (hidden by `when="pool_ok"`) so that both ids are known; the test that keeps the romance kinds out of the baths and the pool, and the one beside it that does not offer "Ask them along" with a friend about to leave for Tokyo (7.6); the two girls' rooms in `NIGHT_CARRY_LINES`; `night_club_bg`; the main menu's and the menu ground's picture lookups and the two copied files (today's `veil.png` to `game/gui/menu_ground.png`, today's `gate.png` to `game/gui/title.png`); `bg club`, `bg shibuya night` and `bg shinjuku night` are undefined without their file and defined with an overlay, and without it `night_bg` and `night_club_bg` return the district's own picture; a My Room evening file laid by overlay turns the Evening cell from `night` to `evening`; a blink row laid in a test `faces.json` is used and its absence falls back to `VIEW_BLINK_ALSO`; the stand-in people in all three states of 8.2; a pose installed by test overlay on a revised picture shows; with no icon art, every exit to a yard draws its area's icon exactly as today and no column's cap changes; the names key is in no `config.keymap` list; `make_icon_workflow.py --names` agrees with `ICONS`; **against U0a's dump of `sb_night.rpy` (`baseline_night_names_u0a.json`), every node outside an init block (Label, Say, Menu, Call, Pass, Return, Scene, Show, With, If, Jump and Python alike) keeps its name with an identical `diff_info()`; only the Python nodes of the init blocks U0c2 edited may differ.** **D60, over a generated test overlay that shapes every one of the 35 places** (an 80 px square round each applied point; the storehouse's two ids sharing one), **with a test manifest that puts every picture at revision 2:** `p2_paths`, `p2_rooms`, `p2_hud`, `p2_view`, `p2_saves`, `p2_contracts`, `p2_cards`, `p2_night`, `u2_home` and `phase2_all` pass, and every line that had to be turned into a rule for it is listed in `docs/UI2_PINS.md` with its old and its new wording; lint is clean with the harnesses' own `zz` hotspots registered in shaped places, and each of them is drawn as its button. **On the Courtyard of that overlay:** no ring and no icon is drawn; with the pointer inside a shape the shape is lit and its name opens against the square at its point; with the square focused by `renpy.set_focus` the same name opens in the same place and the shape is lit; pressing `hotshape_<id>` and pressing `hot_<id>` return the same value; a point outside every polygon focuses nothing; where two test shapes overlap the smaller takes the click; with a person's rectangle over a hotspot's square the hotspot is not drawn and its shape takes no click, an action's row is in the column and a look is offered nowhere; a locked exit lights dimmed, says its reason and does nothing; a test `dim` function's reason is said; with names shown every shape is lit and named; with `path_faces` patched to return two people, their faces show beside a shaped exit's name and on an unshaped exit's tile; a front shape takes the click over a person standing inside it, and a back shape takes none under a test pose box or under a test foreground cut-out; a lit and an unlit frame differ measurably on a white test patch and on a black one; a test `hot_look.json` changes the light's strength and its absence leaves the default. **Gates:** with the same sidecar and the manifest at revision 1, and again with a `for_rev` that does not match, the Courtyard draws its buttons as today; a test manifest with `your_room` at revision 2 and a test `hot_dorm.json` holding shapes shapes My Room. **Saves and motion:** a save made on the place view of the shaped Courtyard loads on it; an instance of the shape's class and its focus function pickle; no function transform is added to the place view (the rings' are gone from a shaped place). **Lint** refuses each broken test sidecar by name (a point outside its own shape; a point under a smaller shape; a polygon of two points; a polygon that crosses itself; a shape more than half under smaller ones; a shape smaller than a button; a shaped place with one exit that has no shape and is not under `keep_tile`) and accepts the whole one. `u2_contracts.rpy` carries the line `## smoke-pointer`, since it now hovers. *Harness:* `u2_contracts.rpy`.

#### U0d · Art tools and the campus record, in three sittings (no `.rpy` change, no file under `game/`)

One builder did not finish anything this size in phase 2 (its tool packages were 350 to 600 lines; the tools here are about fifteen, the existing ones of their kind 330 to 540 lines each), and it sits in front of checkpoint A and every paid sitting. So it is three packages, each with its own acceptance; a failure in one does not hold the others.

**U0d1 · The record and the prompts** (beside U0b and U0c; batch 1 needs it). Its row of 10.1; `campus.json` filled from 5.2, **fixed rows and proposed rows apart**, with `look` and `court_side` apart; **every building the chosen Courtyard shows read off it as a fixed fact** (the schoolhouse, the gateway, the two gate lodges, the pagoda, the training hall), and the grey roof outside the Library's far windows placed as the archive wing; the window walls of the four installed rooms read off their pictures; the working sketch drawn (for reviewers, never for approval); `map.txt`; the prompt check with the exact reach of 5.1. *Acceptance:* `check_campus.py` **passes in full on the record** (its fixed facts fit one ground plan; the Library's far wall faces north and names nothing south of it), fails its negative tests (two exits swapped against a fixed fact in a composed test sidecar; the same with its `waived` row removed) and reports, without failing, the same sidecar with the row present, accepts a test layout that differs only from a proposal and reports the proposal it would rewrite, and lists as "waits for batch N" the facts the new pictures must fix (today's Training Yard sides, the two offices on the Staff map, the hall behind today's gate); batch 1's three prompts are generated, each carrying the chosen Courtyard as a reference and the fixed facts in words, and pass `check_prompts.py`, as do the plain Common Room prompt and the School Courtyard prompt, and a window line with a banned word fails; **the maker refuses the staff steps before the garden is chosen, a room before its yard, and an area map before every place on it has its picture, and it writes batch 3a's My Room (before its corridor exists) and batch 9a's two pictures (a house and a room inside it, in one file) without refusing** (K8); every workflow it writes renders two versions; no relight prompt contains "Keep the time of day"; no map prompt contains `stage.txt`'s words; the Training Yard's prompt does not ask for a vending machine or shoe lockers; no file under `game/` changed.

**U0d2 · The installer and its proof** (beside U0b and U0c; needed before batch 0). Its row of 10.1; the pose tooling of 5.12; `install_face.py`'s `faces.json`; keep lists for the pictures of batches 0, 3 and 4, the Common Room's with its `scale`; **`install_redo.py` given two versions reports a verdict for each and installs only the one the owner names; `--compose` writes the facts a layout fixes into `campus.json` and stamps the manifest with the record's hash.** *Acceptance:* `tools/test_art_tools.sh` passes its synthetic cases and gives the right answers on the sixteen test renders (5.4); `install_redo.py --dry` passes both third-test renders and names what is gone on the second test's emptied Common Room; **sharpness is against revision 1:** with `look3_dorm_common_keep` treated as the installed picture and the same render offered as the candidate, step 5 passes, because it measures against the revision-1 picture from git (no new render is needed to prove it, and no tool queues one: K14); a `change` box is left out of steps 3 and 4 and a moved outline inside it is refused; the eight day and hour pairs the game has today are run through `--against` and the relight thresholds recorded; `--dry --against-rev1` measures a render against the place's revision-1 picture from git and installs nothing (the canary's measure, 5.2); `install_pose.py` refuses two overlapping test boxes that can show together, accepts two that cannot (one awake, one asleep, the same person), refuses a test box that covers an exit point of its room and names the point, refuses one that covers a hotspot point the row does not name in `at`, accepts one that covers the hotspot its row names in `at`, refuses two overlapping test boxes in one district for two people `WORLD_AWAY` sends there the same hour, and writes a manifest row; `make_pose_workflow.py` accepts a test row whose place is a district `WORLD_AWAY` sends that person to and refuses one for a district it does not (5.12); **the batch 5 prompt for Nobara contains a height in pixels** (from the keep list's `scale`), and `make_pose_workflow.py` refuses a test row at the pool and, for a row whose `state` is `asleep`, writes the frozen blanket sentence and no figure; with a test picture and layout `--compose` adds its fixed rows to a copy of the record, and a second test layout that contradicts one of them is refused with the fact named, and installed with a `waived` row only when `--waive` names that fact; every tool refuses, in one line, a `game_layout.json` whose hash is out of date (a changed `.rpy` file or a changed `game/layout/*.json`), and reads the file named by `--layout-dump` when one is given; `art_status.py --check` reports a deleted foreground file; no file under `game/` changed. **Added 7 Oct (D57):** `install_face.py --prepare <who> --sprite <file>` cuts the close-up from a staged sprite in place of the game's; `install_face.py` writes the person's eye and mouth boxes to `faces.json` beside the blink row, measured on the sprite it is given; and `install_set.py <who> <folder>` installs a person's six sprites, six side portraits, two overlay pictures and `faces.json` rows in one step, refusing if any of the fourteen pictures is missing, and regenerates `jjk_sprites.rpy`. *Acceptance, added:* on a copy of the tree, a set with one face missing is refused and nothing is written; a full set is installed and every one of the person's image names still resolves.

**U0d3 · The layout tools and batch 0's layouts** **(Built and merged on 12 Oct: Method note 39 says what was built, what the reviews changed and what is left.)** (after U0c2 has merged: it needs `SPOT_PLANNED` and the scaffold's numbers). **Added 11 Oct (5.7a, Method note 33):** it also points `tools/game_layout.py`'s `where()`, `asleep()` and `away()` at the keys the dump really writes (`world.asleep`, `world.away`, `world.homes`, in the dump's shape); today they read top-level keys that do not exist and answer None, so the pose tools see none of the three tables. Its acceptance gains one line: each of the three answers a known cell from the committed dump. **Added 11 Oct, found after the merge:** `check_content.py --all` has reported one finding since U0c2's pictures commit (b056c59), and exits 1 on it; three reviews passed it as clean and the merge did not run the check. It is `tools/smoke/assets/u2_contracts/game/zz_u2c_gen.rpy:6`: the test overlay's `image nade` does not start with `zz` (`--harness-names`). The overlay must use the real name, because it stands for the line `gen_sprite_defs.py` writes (8.2), so the rule gains a way to say so: `--harness-names` honours a `prefixes.json` exception that gives both the name and its file, with one line of the tool's self-test for it. No baseline is rewritten. Its acceptance gains a second line: `check_content.py --all` exits 0 on the tree. `check_layout.py`, `stage_preview.py`, `layout_sheet.py`; the layouts of the chosen Courtyard and Library, every planned hotspot placed and every exit and hotspot traced as a shape (D60: they are the first two shaped places), staged under `tools/art/staged/<stem>/` for L to install at batch 0; `docs/ART_UI2.md` (the owner's page: the hand-off habit of `ART_PHASE2.md`, the verdict words PASS, LOOK, REFUSED and what each means, that every picture comes as two versions to choose between, which sittings have two files, and what the four people's sittings are like). *Acceptance:* `check_layout.py` passes on the two staged layouts and fails its negative tests (an indoor exit on a window; a slot row over a hotspot; a house whose `spare` list is shorter than the record's count of its spare doors, or holds a point under 110 px from an exit or from another spare point, or outside the frame; and each shape rule of 7.3, one test apiece, its counting function giving the same answers as the game's on the same three shapes; a place where two names meet while names are shown; a front shape that is neither inside its foreground outline nor tight to its object); `stage_preview.py` and `layout_sheet.py` draw a shaped place's outlines and a frame with every shape lit and named; `tools/game_layout.py` gains a `shapes(place)` reader; `install_redo.py` refuses a test render in which the wall under a traced shape's outline has moved 9 px, and names the shape; **batch 0's four pictures are staged from their 2752 px renders, never from the 1920 px files the record's `source` names** (5.13); `stage_preview.py` draws the Common Room at evening with three people and matches a game screenshot of the same state closely enough to judge by eye; no file under `game/` changed.

**U0k · The cut-out's edge** (beside U0b and U0c; no game file touched; needed before batch 0c and before the four people and the curses). D58. `tools/keyedge.py` is the step measured on 7 Oct (`cutout_test/keycut.py`), moved in with its reasons written beside each rule: the backdrop colour read at every pixel, so that a pocket the figure encloses is judged against its own shade; pure backdrop is clear wherever it is; the figure is solid from three pixels in; between the two, how much of a pixel is figure is read off the mix of the backdrop and the figure colour just inside it, and the figure's own colour follows exactly; an outline tinted by the backdrop is brought back to the colouring of the figure beside it, never past neutral; a small patch enclosed by the figure that leans to the backdrop's hue is a pocket and is cleared. It needs numpy and scipy and so starts itself again with ComfyUI's Python, as the look-test tools do. `keysprite.py` gains `--edge new|old` (new for a sprite that is not yet in the game, old kept so that any sprite in the game can be rebuilt as it is); `key_out` is not changed. `tools/make_key_tests.py` builds the sixteen exact pictures from the game's sprites (flat pink and green, crisp and soft, the true cut-out known) and `tools/test_keying.sh` scores both edges on them. `tools/cutcheck.py` runs the anime-trained model directly (ComfyUI's Python, the graphics chip, the picture's own shape at 2048 on the long side; it is skipped with a plain note where the model file is absent) and reports backdrop inside what the model calls figure, figure the model does not see, and stray marks. *Acceptance:* on the sixteen pictures, taken four at a time (one backdrop colour, one kind of edge), the new edge leaves no more than 800 visibly wrong pixels on each crisp four and no more than 9,000 on each soft four (it measured 560 to 620 and 6,400 to 7,600; today's keying 15,600 and 35,700), and the test fails if a bound is passed; a rule that eats a figure's own colour fails it (the two cases met on 7 Oct are tests: pink hair on a pink backdrop, a red hood on pink); Maki's render on pink and Yuji's on green key with no strongly tinted pixel left and no pocket left solid; with `--edge old` the tool's output for a kept render is byte for byte what the tool gives today (the old path is today's code, untouched); no file under `game/` changed.

It installs no picture and re-points no scene line (My Room's five are U9's, 8.1), and **it lands before any lit file is installed.** The harness lines it changes are in the ledger (10.3); their rows in `docs/UI2_PINS.md` are written before it starts (D43, K13). *Acceptance and harness:* the standard set and what 5.7a lists.

**The art tools in a wave-1 worktree.** Each of the six packages edits files `tools/art/game_layout.json` is read from, so the committed dump is out of date in every worktree by design. A worktree writes a local copy (`tools/smoke.sh u2_dump --out shots/art/game_layout.json`), runs `check_layout.py`, `check_campus.py` and `stage_preview.py` with `--layout-dump` on it, and never commits the dump; the committed one is regenerated in the owner's folder at each merge (5.4).

Hand-offs expected in wave 1, by ledger: slots moved by L's in-place series change results in `p2_rooms`, `p2_hud` and `p2_paths` (their numbers assumed today's stage); captions changed by U4 are read by `wp7_ui`; `p2_map.rpy:777-782` (U1 changes, U2 fixes).

**L · the in-place layout sittings** (beside wave 1, in the owner's folder, after U0d3; never while a wave is merging). For each of the sixteen rooms redone in place, on today's picture and in one sitting per room or pair of rooms: the floor, the scale, the slots for one to four people, every `SPOT_PLANNED` point of the room, the shape of every exit and hotspot (D60; traced on the picture the room shows that day, and read once its new picture is in), the exit moved onto its painted door (or to the bottom band), the door box in the keep list, and the polygons of 6.2 that can be drawn today (Gojo's office; Yaga's waits for its desk, batch 12); for the girls' corridor, the points of its spare doors (D50). People and things are placed together because the rules tie them: no slot row for one or two people hides a hotspot (6.3), and what a person's rectangle covers drops into the column (4.6). By 5.6's own figure this is about six hours in all, and about two more for the shapes. Each sitting starts by regenerating `tools/art/game_layout.json` and regenerates it again after writing the room's `hot_` and `view_` files, before `check_layout.py` runs (the hash covers those files, 5.4). *Acceptance, per room:* `check_layout.py` (6.3 in full, the indoor-exit rule, the column count, the shape rules of 7.3), the room's stage sheet with its lit frame, lint. **At the trial merge:** `u2_things` and `u2_blend` are rerun on the real sidecars (every one of the 35 places has a thing to click; every indoor exit of an in-place room is on a door box or in the bottom band; the column still fits; each of the sixteen in-place rooms and every composed picture already installed returns its slots from its sidecar, while a new place still on its stand-in and a yard still at revision 1 are listed as "waits for its layout sitting", 6.5), and `install_redo.py --dry` is rerun for every in-place room already at revision 2 or more, against the merged points: batches 3 and 4 prove "nothing moved" only round the points that existed on the day they were installed.

**L · Yuji's new sprites** (D57; after U0k, U0c2 and U0d2, and after batches 0b and 0c are run; before checkpoint B and before any batch that paints Yuji). In the staging folder `redo_yuji/`: the five faces are laid onto the chosen picture (`composite_face.py --no-install`, each lined up on the hair, ears and collar), the six pictures are keyed with the new edge and fitted to the 760×1400 canvas at 173 cm, and `cutcheck.py` and a close-up sheet on a light and a dark ground are read for each. Then the close-up for batch 0c is cut from the staged calm sprite, the owner runs it, and `install_set.py yuji` puts the fourteen pictures and his `faces.json` rows into the game in one step and regenerates `jjk_sprites.rpy`. In the same step his uniform's cloth is put on the ramp the others are on (`cloth_match.py --apply yuji --redrawn`, D59), and `cloth_match.py --check --redrawn` passes for all six. *Checked after it:* the six sprites share one body (outside the face mask the six are identical pixel for pixel); his blink and mouth sit on his eyes and mouth on every face the blink row names; his feet stand on the line the others stand on and his contact shadow is under them; the saves load as before (`load_all_saves.sh`); every harness that shows Yuji passes. The pictures of today's Yuji are kept in git and in `feetfixed/`.

|---|---|---|

#### Wave 3 · U10 integration

Anything in the ledger still open; `ui2_all.rpy` (a fresh game through the prologue, then thirty days on the new campus by a driver that walks into every one of the 35 places by exits and by the map, sleeps in my room, spends a night in each kind of Night place, visits one friend awake and one asleep (in the sleeper's room the column holds no mission tile, whatever missions are open), rides to Harajuku on a Sunday and spends the afternoon, soaks, sits out the sauna, swims, invites, never once bathes in its second fortnight (K17), and loads a save in each new place; then the made save "standing in my room" played on for three days); `u2_saves.rpy` (save and reload in the corridor, in a friend's room at Night with its owner awake and with its owner asleep, on the invite picker, on `tky_meet`, in the sauna, with a kept foreground in dialogue); the main menu layouts the owner did not choose removed (the other two, and `"today"`); `u2_blend`'s slots check on all 35 places that have their picture (6.5); `docs/PLAYTEST.md` rewritten for this overhaul with a stand-ins list that is complete this time (AUDIT B19); `docs/ART_UI2.md` final; `tools/README.md`; the final load report; "As built" notes in this design.

#### The art sittings (L)

One sitting per batch of 5.13, in the owner's folder, never while a wave is merging. Every sitting starts by regenerating `tools/art/game_layout.json` (`tools/smoke.sh u2_dump`), and regenerates it again after an install that writes a file under `game/layout/` (the hash covers those files, 5.4), so the tools work from today's points; a sitting more than a week after the last then runs the canary of 5.2. For a room redone in place: `install_redo.py`, which recuts foregrounds, measures the light rows and updates the manifest; one commit per room set (the day picture with its hour pictures). For a composed picture or a new place: the layout sitting of 5.6, started when the owner says which version they chose, picture, numbers and the facts it fixes in the record in one commit, every planned hotspot placed and every exit and hotspot traced as a shape (D60). Each sitting ends with lint, `test_art_tools.sh`, `check_layout.py`, `check_campus.py`, `art_status.py --check`, the place's layout sheet and stage sheet for the owner, and `p2_view`, `p2_rooms`, `p2_paths` (the last three alone; U0a made them pass on moved points, and U0c2's acceptance proves them on a room whose hour pictures, foreground and poses have gone stale, 10.2). **A batch's files are written when it is due, never ahead (K4, K8):** each prompt is built from the record as it stands that day, with the pictures chosen so far as its references. Batch 0's sitting (L, after checkpoint A) is where `art_batch.py` writes batch 1's files; batch 2's are written when batch 1's three yards are chosen, and so on outwards. **One exception, by the owner's word (7 Oct):** batches 1 and 2 were written that day by the preview route, into `ui2/early/`, and run while the agent build waited: batch 1 from the record as it stood, and batch 2 from a copy of it in which the three yards the owner chose (O35) are fixed from a first-guess layout read off the pictures by eye. `tools/art/staged/early/` holds both workflows, that copy of the record, and a sum of each prompt and reference picture (the sha256 of a prompt's UTF-8 text, or of a file's bytes). Nothing of either batch is installed before batch 0's sitting. **An early picture is judged as any render is, by 5.2's pass rule at its layout sitting.** There the prompt the record then gives is laid beside the early one and what differs is listed in the sitting's note: the references by what they are (the installed Courtyard is the same render refitted; batch 1's early file was the look-test fit, with four black rows top and bottom), any building crop the early run did not have, and the prompt's sentences by diff. An early picture is run again only if a sentence that differs states a fact (a side, a building, a look line) the early prompt lacked or said otherwise and the picture stands against it; a sentence that only names a reference picture does not count. A code package never writes a real sidecar for a composed picture or a new place: it tests with its own under `tools/smoke/assets/<harness>/`.

Mom, Dad, Kurose and Nade. The main menu as designed. | The review of titles, captions and numbers (one list), with the new numbers named on it: a soak refills stamina in full and the sauna cursed energy in full, an hour each; an afternoon together in Tokyo gives Bond 4; a friend's door asks Bond 15 (the storyboard sets the final levels). |

### 10.6 Cut lines

**Nothing is cut for cost** (O21: "Dont, worry about credits, we're going for best possible quality"). The second draft's order of pictures that would wait if credits ran short is gone, and so is its last rung, a single version of each composed picture. What remains are cut lines for **scope** (a package that cannot pass) and for **the owner's time**.

**If a package fails its acceptance twice** (PHASE2's D51), it is cut at its line and the rest ships; the owner is asked before anything they decided is cut:

- U8, the older screens: all of it. They work as they are.
- U9: the faces (commit 2), then the scene swaps (commit 1; batch 30 is then not made).
- U3: the cast shadow; then the per-slot light; then the shadow's lean (opacity and stretch stay); then the others staying on the person view. Never cut: floor, size, measured light, and the asleep staying off the stage.
- U4: looks cut to two lines each; then to one look a place. Never cut: the bed tags.
- U6: the quick menu's icons; then the choice list's move. Never cut: the main menu's three mock-ups (the owner chooses).
- U2: the tint by hour; the half-height marker.
- U5: nothing (the four places the owner named, the invite and the friends in Tokyo are the owner's).

**If a picture cannot be made** (refused twice, by 5.1's count): that picture keeps its fallback (the old picture, the tint for that hour, the standing sprite, or the room without its addition) and the owner is told what and why. That is a failure of one picture, never a saving.

**If the owner wants fewer sittings,** that is theirs to say, at any time: everything after batch 22 is separable (10.4), and a place's evening and night can wait behind its day picture for as long as they like (the tint shows meanwhile). The plan does not propose a list.

**Never cut without the owner:** the four places they named, the Boys' Dormitory and my room in it, the invite, the campus record and the order "pictures first, the map last", the redo of every existing place, a painted evening and night for every place, two versions of every picture, the missing backgrounds, the main menu, the Bond level on doors, everyone in their own room at Night, the baths staying optional, clicking the picture itself (O34).

### 10.7 Risks

| # | risk | what is done about it |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | An old save no longer loads where it was saved | No label deleted, no variable renamed, no migration; every package loads thirty saves (eleven the owner's, snapshotted first; nineteen made on purpose where this overhaul changes things, each defined by what it holds, because the owner's autosaves rotate and on the day this was checked held no Night, no person view and no map opened from my room), and a row passes only with no dead return site; new labels are appended; the loop is not edited at all |
| 2 | Moving my room strands a save, a night or the walk home | The key never changes (K1); every "home" is a teleport by name; the move is one small package, alone, proved on stand-ins before anything depends on it; 4.4's table is its test list, and each of its rows is a made save, not a slot of the owner's |
| 3 | The house rule in the exit validator breaks walking somewhere | It is in U0b, alone, with the reachability check from `dorm`, the full hover sweep and `phase1_all`'s thirty days before any wave starts; the frozen contract functions keep their signatures |
| 4 | A yard composed from words and its neighbour's picture does not agree with that neighbour, so the yards disagree with each other | Batch 1 is the test: three yards, judged by a stated pass rule, before anything further out is bought; 5.2's table says what follows each failure, down to taking the record's facts from the picture for the two yards that cannot be redone in place; a version that contradicts a fixed fact is marked before the owner chooses; the look lines, the reference crops and `check_campus.py` hold either way |
| 5 | A redo in place quietly moves something by a few pixels | `install_redo.py` refuses on windows round every registered point; `install_bg.py` is never used for a redo; the proof can be rerun from git at any time |
| 6 | "Clean" empties a room, or "real" dirties one | `KEEP` + `LOOK3`, proven on two indoor rooms by day; the variants that are not proven (a named addition and an indoor relight, batch 3; an outdoor relight, batch 7; an outdoor redo by day, batch 8; a named change, only in 5.2's fallback) each have a named first test and a fallback (5.1); the banned-word check on the per-picture lines; the keep lists, the vanished-object refusal and the floor measure |
| 7 | Between batches a room shows old-look dependants | K7's four gates, tested with overlays; foregrounds recut by the installer; for the four rooms a scene or a memory names by the hour, the day picture and its hour pictures in one sitting, because those lines bypass the gates (the one known gap, the Common Room until batch 3, is in K7); everywhere else the gates show the tint until the hours are painted |
| 8 | A composed picture puts something where the screen cannot use it | `stage.txt` in every compose prompt; a picture is installable only with a layout that passes `check_layout.py`; two versions of every composed picture, and one more pair from the allowance if neither can pass |
| 9 | Turning harness pins into rules hides a real regression | U0a must show identical verdicts on the untouched tree and one negative test per rule; the hover sweeps run alone at every layout sitting |
| 10 | A yard's icons overflow the column when people stand in front of its doors | No yard goes above seven exits; 4.6's rule is counted by the tool before a yard is installed and by the unedited harness line after; "Train at the posts" falls back to a look |
| 11 | The six-pin, eighteen-word Dormitories view crowds the map | One more pin than today, on a picture composed for six buildings; the overlap and hover sweeps on the planned points in wave 0 and on the measured ones at the sitting; the owner sees it at checkpoint A on the old map |
| 12 | Blending puts something new into every save made mid-scene | K9: nothing is added to the saved sprite; the tree dump and the pickle are compared with the baseline; R10's save test runs unedited |
| 13 | A measured light row makes people look wrong in a room nobody checked | The sampler proposes, a person tunes on the stage sheet, the check bounds the drift; one sheet per place per hour picture is in the review pack; `define sprite_lighting` remains the off switch |
| 14 | A story edit moves a save made inside a story file | Only U9 edits story files: seven image names, My Room's five `scene` lines pointed at the lit room (O47), two call arguments, one new line, attributes. The owner's menu-parked saves are in a file U9 does not open and in `ch2_tour.rpy`, of which it changes one `scene` line no save rests on, so they prove next to nothing here; what covers U9 is the names check over every node of the edited files, against a baseline taken with the owner's `.rpyc`, and seven saves made on purpose inside those files (a menu, a spoken line, a fight, the night out), loaded with no dead return site; two commits, each right the first time; last; skippable |
| 15 | A sprite for Mom arrives and the image is defined twice, or the block figure vanishes before the sprite exists | The stand-ins sit behind an "image not defined" guard that runs after the generated sprites' definitions, from wave 0; the generator's definition takes over when the generator is run, and a file that has landed without it changes nothing; a harness checks all three states |
| 16 | A painted person does not match the sprites beside them in the new look | Three are bought before the other sixty-four (batches 5 and 20) and looked at in the game. If it fails, poses stop there, the 35 of O28 included; the six asleep shapes, which are not figures beside sprites, are judged on their own |
| 17 | The in-place desk and photograph in Yaga's office do not hold | Two tries; then the office is installed without them (a plain redo in place, one render from the allowance) and Yaga sews at the low table the picture already has |
| 18 | Forty-seven sittings is a lot of pressing Run for a beginner, and some are not one file | It is said plainly in section 0, with what added the eighteen (six of them by the owner's own yes, O28; those six have no pause and come last) and the one more that the owner's ruling of 11 Oct adds (batch 47, D61); twelve renders or fewer, and no run over ten; the tool, not the owner, finds faults and says so in plain words, and the owner only ever chooses between two; section 0 and each note say which sittings have two files and what the four people's sittings are like; every batch is useful alone; the order is the owner's and puts first what changes the feel; everything after batch 22 can stop at any point; and nothing more is added without asking (the one question was asked, and the owner said yes) |
| 19 | The art costs more than said | $0.16 a render is the node's own price; the total allows a fifth again for reruns, and two versions of everything make a rerun rarer; the campus is complete at about $35; the Comfy Cloud plan is outside the figure and section 0 says so; the owner has said credits are not the limit (O21), so a rise is told, not negotiated |
| 20 | New places are empty boxes | Each has a person at some hour, a look, and (the baths and the pool) its own action; the Night column gives the dormitories their reason to exist; the schedule is one table the owner can change with a word at checkpoint B |
| 21 | A locked friend's room reads as a dead end in the first days | The door says what it asks (a Bond level, and a low one); the map and the journal show its owner at home; by day its owner is somewhere I can walk to, and the journal's "Go there" walks to them |
| 22 | ComfyUI's Python is missing or changes; the sprite base route is not available | K14: one clear line, code packages are not blocked, an art sitting cannot install without the proof; the owner's cloud plan is active, Dad is made first as the test of the route, and the local node is the fallback if the plan has lapsed by then |
| 23 | Fixed seeds do not reproduce a picture; the look or the record changes after pictures are made | Every approved render is kept as a file; the look is frozen (9.1); a fixed fact in the record changes only if the owner chooses that picture again, and the manifest stamps each picture with the record's hash, so the status list names the pictures a change touches |
| 24 | A reviewer cannot see what an agent saw | `tools/review_pack.sh`; tools and measurements live in `tools/`; the record's sketch, the sheets and the manifest are regenerated, never kept in scratch |
| 25 | The editor (a preview model) changes during the forty-seven sittings, so later rooms differ from earlier ones | The canary at the start of any sitting more than a week after the last (5.2), which is batch 3a's own Common Room workflow run again against the figures recorded that day, both measured against the revision-1 picture; every approved render is kept |
| 26 | Holding a key to see names collides with a key the engine uses | N, not Tab (Tab toggles skipping, which would also switch the hints and the stage's eases off); `u2_contracts` asserts the key is in no `config.keymap` list |
| 27 | A check that is in every package's standard set cannot pass in a worktree because another package owns the thing it needs | Every contract symbol has a wave-0 stub; every new image name, the board's column, the hidden `swim_pool` row with its icon row, and the pose tooling are in wave 0; hotspot ids are frozen as `SPOT_PLANNED`; `check_campus.py` and the indoor-exit rule have stated rules for pictures and rooms not yet done; the prefixes and the `none_ok` row each new name needs are listed with the package that lays the name |
| 28 | Two painted people at one table paint over each other; with a pose for every cell (O28), up to five painted people share a room, and a pose is painted over a door or a thing to click | Named places, made in order: the 35 are grouped by person, so no two poses of one sitting can show together, and each sitting follows the one whose poses it must keep clear of; the tool refuses a box that overlaps another that can show with it, or that covers an exit point, or a hotspot point other than the one object the row is painted at (its `at`; 5.12), so a pose seated at its own table, desk or board is never refused for it; a refused pose is moved one line and run again from the allowance |
| 29 | A paid run aborts part-way (a blocked prompt, a rate limit) | "water mark" is out of the look and on the banned list; no run is over ten paid boxes; fixed seeds, so a second Run in the same session pays only for what did not finish |
| 30 | Two packages lay out the same rooms apart, and each breaks the other's rules at the merge | One hand lays out each in-place room, people and things together (L's in-place series); the code packages test on sidecars of their own; the real ones are checked at the trial merge, and rooms already installed are proved again round the merged points |
| 31 | The art tools work from numbers that are no longer the game's | One committed dump, hashed against the files it came from (`.rpy` and `game/layout/*.json`), regenerated first in every sitting; a stale one is refused in one line; a worktree works from a local copy it never commits (5.4) |
| 32 | A picture, a caption or an offer at the baths, the pool or a bedroom is one the owner would not want, or more is shown of someone asleep than that they are asleep | K16: nobody is drawn or described in the water; no pose is ever made there (the tool refuses); the romance kinds are not offered in those three places; someone asleep is off the stage, so nothing is offered with them, their caption is one word, their bed's line one sentence and their painted shape one frozen sentence; entering a room starts nothing; a word check over the new lines |
| 33 | Something the owner should not see at a checkpoint is "hidden" behind developer mode, which the launcher always turns on | Notes are hidden for everyone; only the stand-in tag follows developer mode, on purpose |
| 34 | The chosen Courtyard shows buildings the record does not have, or today's gate shows one that is gone | Every building in the two chosen pictures is read into the record as a fixed fact (the gate lodges, the archive wing); the gate is composed again from the record and the chosen Courtyard |
| 35 | The map, made last, cannot be made to agree with the finished places | Every fixed fact is a relation on one ground plan, checked at each choice, so the places agree with each other long before a map is tried; the first area map is made alone as the test (batch 21a); the fallback is maps drawn from the record, which agree by construction; a place is never reopened for a map's sake |
| 36 | Someone asleep leaks onto the stage, into an offer, or into the card invite | One filter, in `stage_people`, the function every screen takes its people from (D48); `u2_world`, `u2_blend` and `u2_rooms` check the stage, the offers, the invite and the map; a companion and a story-pinned person are never asleep, so no scene loses its cast |
| 37 | With the Bond level kept on doors, Night is emptier in the first days than it is today (two people to talk to, where there are three) | Said plainly in section 0 ("Doors") and in 4.3; the level is low (15) and is one table entry; everyone at home shows on the map and in the journal; the storyboard sets the final numbers |
| 38 | The hooks left for quests grow into half a quest system | There are three, and each is one clause or already exists (D49); nothing reads them now but the bed tags, the name plates and the map; section 11 names everything that is not built |
| 39 | A later system quietly makes the baths compulsory | K17 is a ground rule, with a grep in every package's content check and a two-run play test |
| 40 | The Tokyo labels collide with the story phase's district scenes, or a harness's thirty days stop on the new screen | The story phase replaces the body of `district_<key>` and keeps the name; under `smoke_autoplay` the screen is never called, and the ledger lists the trips that must keep passing |
| 41 | The owner chooses a version that quietly rewrites a proposal other pictures were going to use | The installer reports each proposal a choice rewrites, and `art_status.py` lists the unmade pictures it touches; nothing already chosen is ever rewritten |
| 42 | A corridor is painted with spare doors nobody can later use | Each spare door's point is measured at the corridor's layout sitting and must pass the exit rules that day (`check_layout.py`), so it is a legal exit before it is needed; the house's limit of seven exits is stated (4.1) |
| 43 | Yuji is seen half changed: in his uniform on one face and his hoodie on another, or blinking where his eyes used to be | His set is installed by one tool in one step that refuses a set with a picture missing, and until then nothing of his changes (D57). His boxes move with the pictures because they are read from `faces.json` |
| 44 | The new edge step eats part of a figure, as two of its rules did while it was being written | The sixteen exact pictures are its test and fail on either fault; it is used only on new sprites, each read on a close-up sheet before it is installed; the sprites already in the game are not keyed again (D58) |
| 45 | The uniform drifts apart again: a later sprite or a painted pose comes back in its own navy | One ramp, in one tool, with a check that fails when a uniform is off it (D59); Yuji's set is matched inside his install; the pose installer measures a painted uniform and says so |
| 46 | With no buttons on the picture a player cannot tell what can be clicked, and a door goes unfound | The name comes with the light; the first two hints say to rest the pointer on things and to hold N; N or "Show names" lights every shape at once with its name; the map and the journal's "Go there" reach every place without a click on the picture; the owner judges it on the Courtyard and the Library at batch 0, before thirty-three more places are traced, and a shimmer on walking in is one word away |
| 47 | A shape is traced loosely, lights the wrong thing, or two shapes fight over a click | The rules of 7.3 in lint and in `check_layout.py` (a point inside its own shape and no other; the smaller on top; none more than half buried); the lit frame of every place is in the sitting's note and the review pack; the light is soft, so an outline a few pixels off does not show |
| 48 | The checks that find a button stop finding it once a place is shaped, at an art sitting, where no harness may be edited | The button is still there, with its id, square, action, label and dimming, only drawn empty (D60); the few lines that read its icon are turned into rules by U0c2 (10.3), which runs the standing set over an overlay that shapes all 35 places before any real shape exists |
| 49 | A building I can see will not click because someone's picture covers its point, and a thing to look at cannot be reached at all while it is covered | Said to the owner as it is (section 0); a point is put beside the standing rows, and 6.3's rule keeps every hotspot clear of the rows for one and two people; with three or four, a way out or a thing to do is in the column, as today, and a look waits; loosening the rule is named in 7.3 as not done, and the owner is asked at checkpoint B whether it is missed |
| 50 | Batches 1 and 2 were run before batch 0 was installed (the owner's choice of 7 Oct, to use the days the agent build waited), batch 2 from facts read off the chosen yards by eye | The early files, the copy of the record batch 2 was composed from and a sum of every prompt and reference are kept (`tools/art/staged/early/`); each early picture is judged by 5.2's pass rule at its own layout sitting, as any render is, with its early prompt laid beside the day's and the differing sentences listed (10.2); one that stands against a fact it was not told is run again (two renders) |

## 11. What is left to later phases

**Combat (next):** the fight screen; tools and talismans that do something; curses by grade; cut-ins; more talismans.

**Story:** scenes staged on each room's floor plan and at their own hour; a closer view in one-to-one talks; the others staying in the room during the conversation itself; a motion kit and the Last Light grey-out everywhere it is used; painted moments (the storyboard's and the scene list's); the paper and phone views; the storyboard's nine places and its cast, Sukuna in Yuji's body first, with the cheek-mouth; the Divine Dog as a figure of its own (row 40 of 7.2 paints one from words inside Megumi's pose; that pose is remade when the dog has a sprite) and Yaga's doll; "you" to "I"; faces in chapters 4 and 5, the Nade missions, Kurose and Oda, and second faces and speaker tags for Mom, Kurose and Nade; the four "stay the night" scenes set in the new rooms; Tokyo shops to buy from and buildings to walk into; the districts' own scenes (they replace the body of the `district_<key>` labels of 7.6 and keep their names); the club, the day trips and the Gojo night as real scenes; the driver painted into the car; Nade's doorstep; the seller and the friend at the market; reward lines over the Last Light lamp; the town scenes moved onto the Shibuya and Harajuku pictures; the lake, the basement, Yaga's workshop, an armory room, a main-hall interior; the line of chapter 2 that calls the gate "a torii older than the idea of Japan having a capital" (`ch2_tour.rpy:64`), now that the gate is a gatehouse in a garden (O36); and the owner's thought of a larger veil on another mountain, as a locked place for a later arc (O36).

**Left out on purpose by the owner's rulings of 6 and 7 Oct** (each named so that nobody builds it by accident): *quests that use the rooms* (O18): no quest, no item placed in a room, nothing to take, no stealing, nobody who notices, wakes or catches me, no stealth; the three hooks of D49 are all that exists. *Rooms for the characters still to come* (O22): Nanami, Mei Mei, Miwa, Todo, Kamo and others get no room, schedule or sprite now; the spare doors are painted, and 4.1 says how a room is added. *Tokyo* (O23): no shop to buy from, no building to walk into, no district to walk round, nobody riding in with me, no day out I can propose. *The story and the storyboard's cast* (O27): after the world is complete.

**Cut from what earlier drafts of this plan promised** (second verification; each because the plan could not deliver it as written): notes "shown while developer mode is on" (they are hidden for everyone and stay in the source as comments); a painted person at the baths or the pool "whenever wanted" (never: K16); friends at home by day more than one weekend hour each (a schedule cell each, whenever wanted; the doors themselves are open at every hour once Bond allows); the rooftop in the first paid sitting (it waits for batch 8, where the outdoor wording is first tried).

**Whenever wanted, each small:** chess, memory, tic-tac-toe; watching the television with someone, the console, fishing with a friend; outfits; reusable cut-outs; activity pictures; a talking mouth per face; Sana's blink; item, fish and computer-page pictures; a painted card table; a bigger grade card; the hour and yen on the HUD; text size; a second foreground plane; an open map area kept across a save; a blink and a talking mouth on every face; a picture of its own for each of the board's eight jobs.

**Its own pass:** music and sound, credits, a gallery, accessibility. **Not planned:** video (one $1 test whenever the owner likes); group pictures.
